Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the quantum_mechanics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^352 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 528-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
